# Portrait credits

The map uses low-resolution portrait thumbnails so that each dot can be recognised at a glance. The files are stored locally in `avatars/` and are not hot-linked.

Most portraits come from Wikipedia/Wikimedia Commons; the rest are IMDb profile thumbnails. Copyright and other rights remain with the original photographers, studios, publishers and estates. These small, unmodified-for-identification copies are used here to illustrate the people named in the gossip list; they are not licensed for reuse.

Generated placeholder avatars were used where no reliable portrait was found (9 of 1903). Susanna Eng is a known hoax and is included only to show what the list claimed; her generated avatar is not a portrait of a real person.
